          "type": "text",
          "text": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory\\n\\n## 项目定位\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\n\\n## 执行约束\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\n\\n## 领域参考\\n\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\n\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\n\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\n\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\n\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\n\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\n\\n## 首批实验协议\\n\\n### 统一评测输出\\n\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\n\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\n\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\n\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\n\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\"。\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\n\\n## Real-data 可行性\\n\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali ho\\n\\n... 未通过 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` registry success rule。\n\n这不等于证伪 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 的 single-DGP counterexample。`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 仍支持单点 worst-case 机制；`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 证伪的是当前 grid `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 指标解释了为什么 exp001 success 与 exp002 negative 可以同时成立：它们不是同一 algebra/weighting/aggregation problem。\n\n## 证伪粒度\n\n结论不是 \"完全不 work\"。`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` condition-level artifact 显示局部条件仍会出现 oracle global worse than uncorrected，但这些局部模式没有按 positive-vs-negative correlation 形成 grid-level separation。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` matched positive-negative pair audit：matched pairs = 96；positive-minus-negative `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = -0.004999998636776581；positive-delta pairs = 36；zero-delta pairs = 2；negative-delta pairs = 58。该证据说明有效性是局部的，而不是 H1 grid-level monotone pattern。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` condition-level mean RMSE audit：mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse > uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96，mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse <= uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96。worse-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.39499998092651367, 0.9799999594688416]；better/equal-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.014999999664723873, 0.4599999785423279]。\n\n局部有效 pattern 主要是 low CATE heterogeneity，而不是 positive correlation 本身：`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` low-CATE conditions 的 positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.6684、negative mean = 0.6742；high-CATE conditions 的 positive mean = 0.2157、negative mean = 0.2200。也就是说，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 的 \"global worse\" 更受 CATE heterogeneity scale 与 sampling noise/normalization 影响，而不是受 positive-vs-negative direction 主导。\n\n更细的 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` pattern：\n\n- `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.8404，negative mean = 0.8554；这类条件 global correction 常变差，但 negative controls 同样或更强。\n- `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.8483，negative mean = 0.8612；同样是 high worse-rate but no positive-specific separation。\n- `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.3333，negative mean = 0.3221；这是少数 positive direction 稍高的 aggregate pattern，但幅度远低于 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。\n- `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=3`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.1192，negative mean = 0.1283；在最接近 \"high/high\" intuition 的 grid cell 中，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 反而不支持 global-worse pattern。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` top matched positive deltas 也显示局部 pattern 不稳定：最大 positive-minus-negative pair delta = 0.09999999403953552，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=5000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.515、negative rate = 0.415。最大 negative delta = -0.0949999988079071，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=10000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.295、negative rate = 0.390。两者都不是 high/high mechanism 的 clean confirmation。\n\n## exp001-vs-exp002 差异\n\nParameter construction differs materially. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` uses a deliberately skewed worst-case DGP: `tau_values=[0.0, 4.3, 5.4, 5.5]`、`error_rates=[0.02, 0.06, 0.29, 0.45]`、`group_probs=[0.59, 0.09, 0.14, 0.18]` from `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses uniform true subgroup sampling, CATE ranges centered near 0.5, misclassification ranges `low=[0.10,0.18]` and `high=[0.04,0.36]`, and `k in {3,6}` from `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`. The exp001 single point creates severe prior-weighted posterior distortion; exp002 dilutes that into many regimes where uncorrected noise can already be close to the target or global correction acts like a shrinkage transform.\n\nCorrection algebra differs and is the strongest mechanism-audit finding. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` constructs `posterior_true_given_noisy = P(G | Z)` using both confusion and subgroup prior, then solves `tau_Z = W tau_G` with a pseudo-inverse. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` computes `observed_gate` by noisy labels but then solves `transition.T * tau = observed_gate` directly. For subgroup-varying error rates, observed GATE by noisy label is a posterior-weighted estimand that requires normalization by noisy-label prevalence. Directly inverting `transition.T` is not algebraically equivalent to the exp001 posterior correction, except in special balanced cases. This can flip or mute the intended under/over-correction mechanism.\n\nSubgroup weighting differs. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` global confusion uses a group-probability-weighted pooled error, so high-effect minority groups can be over/under-corrected relative to a majority-driven pooled matrix. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses `z_true = torch.randint(0, k, ...)`, so subgroup priors are uniform in expectation and the global pooled error is an unweighted mean of subgroup error rates. That removes a large part of exp001's prior-weighted worst-case leverage.\n\nMetric aggregation differs. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` primary metric is paired mean `oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse` in one positive high/high DGP. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` primary metric is the difference of mean `oracle_global_worse_rate` between positive and negative condition sets. A condition can have a small positive mean RMSE delta but a high worse-rate, or a negative mean RMSE delta but a nonzero worse-rate. This makes exp002 a direction-discrimination test, not a direct repeat of exp001.\n\nPositive/negative correlation definition differs from the exp001 mechanism. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` defines direction by ranking `error_rates` against `abs(cate)`, but its `bias_bound_score` uses `abs(centered_rate * centered_cate)`, which discards sign. Therefore positive and negative directions can share the same or nearly same diagnostic score. This explains why `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` bias-bound Spearman can be negative even when local positive cells exist: the diagnostic measures magnitude coupling, not signed positive-vs-negative coupling.\n\nOracle-vs-estimated split does not explain exp002 negative. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap is 0.0007658914546482265, while the primary failure is -0.004999995231628418 and the rank diagnostic is -0.6201611161231995. The exp002 failure is therefore not caused by estimated confusion matrix budget noise.\n\nThe stratified subgroup comparator is also a warning sign. In `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`, stratified correction has `gate_rmse=0.08234754252792217` versus uncorrected `gate_rmse=0.26032738576584474`. In `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`, aggregated stratified-minus-uncorrected mean is positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high` at 0.03885 and positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low` at 0.07807. Since the stratified comparator uses the true subgroup transition but the same direct `transition.T` solve, this supports the algebra-mismatch explanation.\n\n## 建议 hypothesis\n\nH1a: If correction is implemented with a posterior-normalized operator `P(G | Z)` and the same subgroup-prior weighting as the estimand, then positive misclassification-CATE correlation will again separate from negative controls in the exp002 grid. This should be tested before interpreting the current exp002 negative as a substantive failure of the EC-HTE mechanism.\n\nH1b: The exp001 mechanism requires both signed positive error-CATE coupling and subgroup-prior imbalance. A balanced-prior grid may not show global correction worse than uncorrected unless the design explicitly varies group probabilities and reports prior-weighted posterior distortion.\n\nH1c: `bias_bound_score` should be signed or decomposed into signed correlation and magnitude terms. A magnitude-only diagnostic cannot distinguish positive and negative correlation controls and should not be the secondary success criterion for H1 directionality.\n\nH1d: Stage1 correction ablations should remain blocked until the Stage0 correction operator is aligned. Otherwise partial pooling may be judged against an algebraically inconsistent \"stratified\" or \"global\" correction baseline.\n\nNo experiment was created or launched for these hypotheses in this audit.\n\n## Sources read\n\n- Registry entries: `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`, `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`, `exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation`.\n- Project files: `ROADMAP.md`, `memory.md`.\n- Remote artifacts read over SSH: `/home/root/ec_hte/artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/summary.json`, `/home/root/ec_hte/artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/planned_metrics.json`, `/home/root/ec_hte/artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/condition_metrics.jsonl`, `/home/root/ec_hte/artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/train.log`, `/home/root/ec_hte/artifacts/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/formal/summary.json`.\n- Scripts/config inspected: `repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/run_experiment.py`, `repro/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/run_gpu_dgp_grid.py`, `configs/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid.json`.\n",

    "aggregated_output": "# exp002 negative literature solution memo\n\nDate: 2026-05-31\nProject: ec_hte_codex_a\nTarget experiment: `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`\n\n## Executive decision\n\nDo not launch formal Stage 1 from the current exp002 chain yet. The negative result most likely indicates that the current grid criterion and correction algebra are not isolating the intended heterogeneous-misclassification mechanism. The next useful step is a short mechanism-audit experiment, not a broader H2 run.\n\nPriority path:\n\n1. Replace the direct `P(Z_hat | Z)^T` inverse used for GATE correction with the posterior mixing matrix `P(Z | Z_hat)` or an equivalent prevalence-aware likelihood/EM formulation.\n2. Recompute the H1 grid diagnostic using directional, signed subgroup error terms rather than the current magnitude-only bias-bound score.\n3. Then test partial pooling only on correction matrices that recover the right estimand; otherwise partial pooling will stabilize the wrong algebra.\n\n## Registry and roadmap confirmation\n\nRegistry hypothesis for `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`: a GPU-vectorized DGP grid varying subgroup misclassification heterogeneity and CATE heterogeneity should reproduce the EC-HTE bias-bound mechanism: positive correlation between error and treatment effect should make global correction worse than uncorrected annotations, while negative correlation should not show the same degradation.\n\nOperational H1 grid criterion from `ROADMAP.md`:\n\n- Primary metric: `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`.\n- Success: positive-correlation conditions' oracle-global-worse rate must be at least 20 percentage points higher than negative-correlation controls, with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0.\n- Secondary diagnostic: `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` must be >= 0.50, with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0.\n- Estimated-global must be reported separately from oracle-global.\n\nObserved negative result, source `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` registry and `artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/planned_metrics.json`:\n\n- `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative = -0.005`, 95% CI `[-0.085, 0.080]`.\n- `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman = -0.620`, 95% CI `[-0.665, -0.483]`.\n- `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse = 0.000766`.\n\nLocal condition-metric aggregation from `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` shows the negative Spearman is driven more by grid axes than by positive-vs-negative direction: mean oracle-global-worse rate was 0.671 for low CATE heterogeneity and 0.218 for high CATE heterogeneity; mean rates were 0.442 for positive and 0.447 for negative. This supports a mechanism-audit interpretation rather than \"H1 is globally false.\"\n\n## Failure modes implied by exp002\n\n### F1. Matrix orientation / estimand mismatch\n\nThe exp002 code constructs a row-stochastic transition matrix `C[g,h] = P(Z_hat=h | Z=g)` and applies `solve(C.T, observed_gate)`. For subgroup means/GATEs indexed by observed labels, however, the observed vector is mixed by `P(Z=g | Z_hat=h)`, which depends on subgroup prevalence and column normalization. Directly inverting `C.T` is only harmless in special cases such as uniform prevalence plus doubly stochastic error. Heterogeneous row errors break that condition.\n\nLiterature link: matrix/inverse matrix methods are estimand-specific and sensitive to the assumed misclassification model; applying a nondifferential or wrong correction can be worse than the naive estimate.\n\n### F2. Direction-only H1 criterion is too coarse\n\nThe positive-vs-negative criterion expects the sign of error-CATE correlation to predict global-correction failure. In the current grid, global correction uses an average error rate and symmetric off-diagonal flips. With this DGP, CATE spread, subgroup count, and sample size dominate direction: high CATE heterogeneity produced lower global-worse rates than low CATE heterogeneity in the exp002 condition metrics.\n\n### F3. Finite-sample expert-label noise is not the main failure\n\nThe estimated-global minus oracle-global GATE RMSE gap is only 0.000766 in `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`. The failure is therefore not primarily due to expert budget estimation noise. More labels would not fix the current H1 diagnostic or matrix algebra.\n\n### F4. Soft/probabilistic membership is not automatically enough\n\nLatent subgroup and classifier-calibration literatures warn that using hard labels ignores classification uncertainty, but also that soft assignment can still preserve bias if the membership probabilities are not tied to the causal estimand or sensitivity model. For EC-HTE, calibrated LLM probabilities should feed a posterior-mixing or likelihood correction, not merely replace hard subgroup labels in a causal forest.\n\n## Source-linked solution patterns\n\n### 1. Extended matrix / inverse matrix correction with validation data\n\nSource: Tang, Lyles & Ye, \"Extended Matrix and Inverse Matrix Methods Utilizing Internal Validation Data When Both Disease and Exposure Status Are Misclassified\", Epidemiologic Methods 2013. DOI: 10.1515/em-2013-0008. URL: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/\n\nRelevance: Directly matches EC-HTE's use of internal validation labels to correct categorical misclassification. The paper is useful because it emphasizes that matrix correction must match the data-generating misclassification structure, and that an incorrect nondifferential-error assumption can perform worse than the naive estimate.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: use internal validation data to estimate the appropriate correction matrix; separate matrix, inverse-matrix, and likelihood corrections; check whether the assumed nondifferential matrix is valid before applying the inverse.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: global confusion correction should be treated as a model class with explicit assumptions, not as a default debiaser. Add a diagnostic that compares row-stochastic `P(Z_hat|Z)` correction against posterior `P(Z|Z_hat)` correction under known prevalence.\n\n### 2. Hierarchical semi-Bayes partial pooling for sparse validation strata\n\nSource: Dement et al., \"Hierarchical Semi-Bayes Methods for Misclassification in Perinatal Epidemiology\", Epidemiology 2018. DOI: 10.1097/EDE.0000000000000789. URL: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5792373/\n\nRelevance: New source beyond memory.md. This is the closest off-the-shelf template for subgroup-specific misclassification correction when validation labels are sparse. It explicitly addresses stratum-specific predictive values that may vary by covariates but are hard to estimate independently.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: fit a hierarchical model for bias parameters/predictive values, shrink stratum estimates toward a grand mean, then propagate posterior draws into the outcome model through probabilistic bias analysis. The two-step semi-Bayes version avoids the convergence cost of a fully joint Bayesian outcome-plus-error model.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: partial pooling should pool confusion-matrix logits or predictive probabilities across subgroup/error strata. It should be compared against both global and independent per-subgroup matrices only after the correction target is made prevalence-aware.\n\n### 3. Misclassified biomarker subgroup treatment effects via mixture/EM\n\nSource: Wan, Titman & Jaki, \"Subgroup Analysis of Treatment Effects for Misclassified Biomarkers with Time-to-Event Data\", JRSS Series C 2019. DOI: 10.1111/rssc.12364. arXiv: https://arxiv.org/abs/1708.00942. Publisher URL: https://academic.oup.com/jrsssc/article/68/5/1447/7058601\n\nRelevance: New source beyond memory.md. This is directly about treatment-effect subgroup analysis when the subgroup-defining biomarker is misclassified. It argues that simple adjustment is invalid for survival subgroup effects and instead models true subgroup membership as latent.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: formulate the observed subgroup data as a mixture over true subgroup status; estimate subgroup treatment effects with an EM algorithm or corrected-score approach; use simultaneous intervals for subgroup effects.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: implement a likelihood/EM baseline where true modifier class is latent, observed LLM class is noisy, and treatment-specific outcomes identify GATEs through posterior class weights. This is the cleanest alternative to vector inverse correction.\n\n### 4. MC-SIMEX for categorical misclassification\n\nSource: Küchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, \"A general method for dealing with misclassification in regression: the misclassification SIMEX\", Biometrics 2006. DOI: 10.1111/j.1541-0420.2005.00396.x. URL: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/\n\nRelevance: Already represented in memory.md, but important here because exp002's failure suggests a need for a diagnostic that perturbs categorical labels through a known transition matrix and tests whether extrapolation recovers the intended bias direction.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: add controlled extra misclassification according to a transition matrix, fit the downstream estimator repeatedly, then extrapolate back to the no-error limit.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: use MC-SIMEX as a diagnostic baseline, not primary solution. If MC-SIMEX and posterior-matrix correction disagree under oracle transition matrices, the DGP/estimand implementation is not yet stable enough for Stage 1.\n\n### 5. Classification uncertainty in latent subgroup effect analysis\n\nSource: Loh & Kim, \"Evaluating sensitivity to classification uncertainty in latent subgroup effect analyses\", BMC Medical Research Methodology 2022. DOI: 10.1186/s12874-022-01720-8. URL: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9508766/\n\nRelevance: New source beyond memory.md. It targets subgroup-specific causal effects when subgroup membership is imputed or probabilistic, which closely parallels LLM-derived modifiers.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: routinely perform sensitivity analysis over classification uncertainty, including graphical/numerical summaries, instead of treating imputed subgroup labels as fixed. The paper also warns that soft/proportional assignment can still carry classification bias.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: add a \"membership uncertainty stress test\" for each correction method: perturb calibrated posterior class probabilities within validation-supported ranges and report GATE ranking stability, not only GATE RMSE.\n\n### 6. Multiclass probability calibration for soft modifiers\n\nSource: Kull et al., \"Beyond temperature scaling: Obtaining well-calibrated multi-class probabilities with Dirichlet calibration\", NeurIPS 2019. arXiv: https://arxiv.org/abs/1910.12656. Proceedings URL: https://papers.nips.cc/paper/9397-beyond-temperature-scaling-obtaining-well-calibrated-multi-class-probabilities-with-dirichlet-calibration\n\nRelevance: New source beyond memory.md. EC-HTE needs calibrated LLM class probabilities if it will use soft modifier membership or posterior mixing.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: use natively multiclass Dirichlet calibration rather than one-vs-rest binary calibration when the modifier has k > 2 classes; evaluate calibration with classwise ECE, log-loss, and Brier score.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: LLM modifier calibration should output calibrated `P(Z | text, metadata)` and then feed posterior-mixing/latent-class correction. Do not evaluate it only by hard-label accuracy.\n\n### 7. Temperature scaling and calibration diagnostics\n\nSource: Guo et al., \"On Calibration of Modern Neural Networks\", ICML 2017. arXiv: https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.04599\n\nRelevance: Gives a simple calibration baseline for probabilistic modifiers. It is less structurally matched than Dirichlet calibration but useful as a low-cost baseline.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: learn a held-out scalar temperature for logits and evaluate expected calibration error / negative log-likelihood.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: include temperature scaling as a weak baseline; expect it to help overconfidence but not subgroup-specific confusion asymmetry.\n\n### 8. CATE calibration as a separate target\n\nSource: van der Laan et al., \"Causal Isotonic Calibration for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects\", PMLR 2023. PMCID: PMC10416780. URL: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10416780/\n\nRelevance: New source beyond memory.md. It separates calibrating treatment-effect predictions from calibrating class labels.\n\nDesign choice/trick from others: cross-fit CATE predictors and calibrate predicted HTE values nonparametrically, avoiding a simple holdout-only calibration loss.\n\nEC-HTE mapping: after modifier-error correction, calibrate the resulting CATE/GATE predictor separately. This should not replace membership-error correction, but it can catch over/understated effect magnitudes after correction.\n\n## Candidate next hypotheses\n\n### H-next-001: posterior-matrix correction\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: In the exp002 DGP, replacing the current direct inverse of `P(Z_hat | Z)^T` with a prevalence-aware posterior mixing correction based on `P(Z | Z_hat)` will make oracle subgroup-specific correction improve GATE RMSE over uncorrected in high-error/high-CATE conditions, while the current direct inverse will not.\n\nResponds to failure mode: F1 matrix orientation / estimand mismatch.\n\nTest: rerun a small subset of exp002 cells with three oracle methods: current `C.T` inverse, posterior-mixing inverse, and likelihood/EM latent-class correction. Success requires posterior or EM correction to beat uncorrected and current inverse on GATE RMSE with bootstrap CI upper bound for method-minus-current < 0.\n\n### H-next-002: likelihood/EM latent modifier model\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: When subgroup membership is misclassified and treatment effects differ by true subgroup, a latent-class outcome model using the observed LLM label as a noisy proxy will recover subgroup GATEs with lower RMSE than hard-label uncorrected and global confusion correction because it estimates treatment interactions over posterior true-class weights.\n\nResponds to failure modes: F1 and F4.\n\nTest: implement binary/k-class EM baseline on synthetic cells with known transition matrix and uniform/nonuniform prevalences. Success requires EM GATE RMSE < global correction GATE RMSE in positive and negative correlation settings, not only in one hand-picked cell.\n\n### H-next-003: directional bias-bound diagnostic\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: A signed diagnostic that estimates the covariance between subgroup-specific correction residuals and subgroup CATE direction will predict oracle-global-worse conditions better than the current magnitude-only `mean(abs(centered_rate * centered_abs_cate))` score, because global correction error depends on the direction of contamination in the GATE vector.\n\nResponds to failure mode: F2.\n\nTest: compute candidate signed diagnostics from existing `condition_metrics.jsonl`, then confirm on a repaired DGP grid. Success requires Spearman rho >= 0.50 with bootstrap CI lower > 0 for the repaired diagnostic; otherwise H1 should be reframed around estimator-specific algebra rather than error-CATE correlation alone.\n\n### H-next-004: hierarchical confusion-matrix pooling\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: Under sparse expert validation labels, hierarchical partial pooling over subgroup confusion-matrix logits will reduce GATE RMSE relative to independent per-subgroup confusion matrices by lowering variance, while still improving over global correction when true subgroup errors are heterogeneous.\n\nResponds to failure modes: F3 and the blocked H2 path.\n\nTest: after H-next-001 passes, run low-budget cells B in {100, 500} with global, independent subgroup, and hierarchical subgroup correction. Success follows ROADMAP H2: partial-pooling-minus-global bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0 and within 5% relative GATE RMSE of independent correction.\n\n### H-next-005: calibrated soft modifier correction\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: Dirichlet-calibrated multiclass LLM modifier probabilities used inside a posterior-mixing correction will reduce subgroup GATE bias more than hard-label confusion correction when confusion is asymmetric across classes, because calibrated probabilities preserve uncertainty about near-boundary records that hard labels discard.\n\nResponds to failure mode: F4.\n\nTest: simulate LLM score vectors with controllable miscalibration; compare hard-label confusion, temperature scaling, Dirichlet calibration, and oracle probabilities. Success requires lower GATE RMSE and lower classwise calibration error for Dirichlet/posterior correction than hard-label correction.\n\n### H-next-006: MC-SIMEX as a robustness screen\n\nMechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: If the corrected H1 mechanism is real, MC-SIMEX extrapolation using the oracle categorical transition matrix will move GATE estimates toward the true GATE vector as added misclassification increases and extrapolates back, whereas the current exp002 inverse correction may move estimates in the wrong direction under heterogeneous row errors.\n\nResponds to failure modes: F1 and F2.\n\nTest: run MC-SIMEX on a fixed subset of exp002 cells. Success requires extrapolated GATE RMSE < uncorrected GATE RMSE in the same cells where posterior-matrix correction succeeds.\n\n## Concrete implementation recommendations\n\n1. Add a prevalence-aware correction function:\n   - Estimate `C[g,h] = P(Z_hat=h | Z=g)` from validation data.\n   - Estimate subgroup prevalence `pi_g`.\n   - Compute observed-label prevalence `q_h = sum_g pi_g C[g,h]`.\n   - Compute posterior mixing `M[h,g] = pi_g C[g,h] / q_h`.\n   - Solve `observed_gate = M true_gate` rather than `observed_gate = C.T true_gate`.\n\n2. Keep the current direct inverse only as a negative-control baseline named `transition_transpose_inverse`, not as \"oracle subgroup correction.\"\n\n3. Add nonuniform subgroup prevalence cells. A correction method that only works under uniform prevalence is not acceptable for real LLM annotation settings.\n\n4. Add asymmetric off-diagonal confusion. Uniformly spreading errors to all other classes makes class destination irrelevant; real categorical LLM errors are usually structured.\n\n5. Split diagnostics:\n   - matrix recovery error,\n   - observed-label posterior calibration,\n   - GATE RMSE,\n   - subgroup bias direction,\n   - ranking stability under membership uncertainty.\n\n6. Make calibrated probabilities a correction input, not an endpoint. Report ECE/log-loss/Brier plus downstream GATE RMSE.\n\n## Director-facing recommendation\n\nDecision: pause formal H2 launch. Approve a narrow mechanism-audit patch around H-next-001 through H-next-003. If posterior/EM correction repairs the grid-level relationship, then resume H2 partial-pooling using the repaired correction target. If it does not, revise H1 from \"positive error-CATE correlation makes global correction worse\" to a sharper estimator-specific claim: global correction fails when the posterior observed-label mixing matrix differs materially from the pooled correction matrix in directions aligned with subgroup treatment-effect contrasts.\n\nNo findings.md update was made. No experiment was launched.\n",
    "exit_code": 0,
    "status": "completed"
  }
}

confusion-matrix\n./registry.yaml:304:      unit-level confusion-matrix experiment is not currently supported because anonymized\n./ROADMAP.md:3:> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\n./ROADMAP.md:9:核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n./ROADMAP.md:13:H1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\n./ROADMAP.md:15:H2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n./ROADMAP.md:17:H3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n./ROADMAP.md:25:H1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\n./ROADMAP.md:27:H1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\n./ROADMAP.md:29:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\n./ROADMAP.md:31:H1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\n./ROADMAP.md:37:1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\n./ROADMAP.md:38:2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\n./ROADMAP.md:39:3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\n./ROADMAP.md:40:4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\n./ROADMAP.md:46:- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\n./ROADMAP.md:47:- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\n./ROADMAP.md:71:- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\n./ROADMAP.md:104:| D004 | 2026-05-31 | Accept exp002 literature solutions memo as advisor | exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid, exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation |\n./ROADMAP.md:105:| D003 | exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\n./artifacts/manifest.yaml:201:  - H1\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:5:被证伪的是 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 的 grid-level operational hypothesis：在系统变化 subgroup misclassification heterogeneity 与 CATE heterogeneity 后，positive correlation between error and treatment effect 应使 oracle global confusion correction 比 uncorrected annotations 更常变差，而 negative-correlation controls 不应同样退化。该 hypothesis 未通过 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` registry success rule。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:7:这不等于证伪 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 的 single-DGP counterexample。`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 仍支持单点 worst-case 机制；`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 证伪的是当前 grid construction、correction algebra 与 aggregation criterion 下的泛化版本。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:11:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` formal artifact 显示：conditions = 192、replications_per_condition = 200、device = cuda、elapsed_seconds = 51.99，均来自 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` summary/registry。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:13:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` primary metric `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative` = -0.004999995231628418，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.08526039123535156, 0.08000001311302185]。它没有达到 positive conditions 至少高出 negative controls 20 percentage points 且 CI lower bound > 0 的 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:15:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` secondary metric `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` = -0.6201611161231995，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.6645349264144897, -0.4831351935863495]。方向与 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule 要求的 positive rank correlation 相反。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:17:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse` = 0.0007658914546482265。该数值说明当前 negative result 主要不是 expert-budget finite-sample estimation noise，而是 oracle/global algebra 与 DGP/metric interaction 的问题。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:23:结论不是 \"完全不 work\"。`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` condition-level artifact 显示局部条件仍会出现 oracle global worse than uncorrected，但这些局部模式没有按 positive-vs-negative correlation 形成 grid-level separation。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:25:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` matched positive-negative pair audit：matched pairs = 96；positive-minus-negative `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = -0.004999998636776581；positive-delta pairs = 36；zero-delta pairs = 2；negative-delta pairs = 58。该证据说明有效性是局部的，而不是 H1 grid-level monotone pattern。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:27:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` condition-level mean RMSE audit：mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse > uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96，mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse <= uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96。worse-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.39499998092651367, 0.9799999594688416]；better/equal-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.014999999664723873, 0.4599999785423279]。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:29:局部有效 pattern 主要是 low CATE heterogeneity，而不是 positive correlation 本身：`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` low-CATE conditions 的 positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.6684、negative mean = 0.6742；high-CATE conditions 的 positive mean = 0.2157、negative mean = 0.2200。也就是说，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 的 \"global worse\" 更受 CATE heterogeneity scale 与 sampling noise/normalization 影响，而不是受 positive-vs-negative direction 主导。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:31:更细的 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` pattern：\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:35:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.3333，negative mean = 0.3221；这是少数 positive direction 稍高的 aggregate pattern，但幅度远低于 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:36:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=3`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.1192，negative mean = 0.1283；在最接近 \"high/high\" intuition 的 grid cell 中，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 反而不支持 global-worse pattern。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:38:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` top matched positive deltas 也显示局部 pattern 不稳定：最大 positive-minus-negative pair delta = 0.09999999403953552，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=5000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.515、negative rate = 0.415。最大 negative delta = -0.0949999988079071，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=10000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.295、negative rate = 0.390。两者都不是 high/high mechanism 的 clean confirmation。\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:42:Parameter construction differs materially. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` uses a deliberately skewed worst-case DGP: `tau_values=[0.0, 4.3, 5.4, 5.5]`、`error_rates=[0.02, 0.06, 0.29, 0.45]`、`group_probs=[0.59, 0.09, 0.14, 0.18]` from `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses uniform true subgroup sampling, CATE ranges centered near 0.5, misclassification ranges `low=[0.10,0.18]` and `high=[0.04,0.36]`, and `k in {3,6}` from `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`. The exp001 single point creates severe prior-weighted posterior distortion; exp002 dilutes that into many regimes where uncorrected noise can already be close to the target or global correction acts like a shrinkage transform.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:44:Correction algebra differs and is the strongest mechanism-audit finding. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` constructs `posterior_true_given_noisy = P(G | Z)` using both confusion and subgroup prior, then solves `tau_Z = W tau_G` with a pseudo-inverse. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` computes `observed_gate` by noisy labels but then solves `transition.T * tau = observed_gate` directly. For subgroup-varying error rates, observed GATE by noisy label is a posterior-weighted estimand that requires normalization by noisy-label prevalence. Directly inverting `transition.T` is not algebraically equivalent to the exp001 posterior correction, except in special balanced cases. This can flip or mute the intended under/over-correction mechanism.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:46:Subgroup weighting differs. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` global confusion uses a group-probability-weighted pooled error, so high-effect minority groups can be over/under-corrected relative to a majority-driven pooled matrix. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses `z_true = torch.randint(0, k, ...)`, so subgroup priors are uniform in expectation and the global pooled error is an unweighted mean of subgroup error rates. That removes a large part of exp001's prior-weighted worst-case leverage.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:48:Metric aggregation differs. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` primary metric is paired mean `oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse` in one positive high/high DGP. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` primary metric is the difference of mean `oracle_global_worse_rate` between positive and negative condition sets. A condition can have a small positive mean RMSE delta but a high worse-rate, or a negative mean RMSE delta but a nonzero worse-rate. This makes exp002 a direction-discrimination test, not a direct repeat of exp001.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:50:Positive/negative correlation definition differs from the exp001 mechanism. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` defines direction by ranking `error_rates` against `abs(cate)`, but its `bias_bound_score` uses `abs(centered_rate * centered_cate)`, which discards sign. Therefore positive and negative directions can share the same or nearly same diagnostic score. This explains why `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` bias-bound Spearman can be negative even when local positive cells exist: the diagnostic measures magnitude coupling, not signed positive-vs-negative coupling.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:52:Oracle-vs-estimated split does not explain exp002 negative. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap is 0.0007658914546482265, while the primary failure is -0.004999995231628418 and the rank diagnostic is -0.6201611161231995. The exp002 failure is therefore not caused by estimated confusion matrix budget noise.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:54:The stratified subgroup comparator is also a warning sign. In `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`, stratified correction has `gate_rmse=0.08234754252792217` versus uncorrected `gate_rmse=0.26032738576584474`. In `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`, aggregated stratified-minus-uncorrected mean is positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high` at 0.03885 and positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low` at 0.07807. Since the stratified comparator uses the true subgroup transition but the same direct `transition.T` solve, this supports the algebra-mismatch explanation.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:58:H1a: If correction is implemented with a posterior-normalized operator `P(G | Z)` and the same subgroup-prior weighting as the estimand, then positive misclassification-CATE correlation will again separate from negative controls in the exp002 grid. This should be tested before interpreting the current exp002 negative as a substantive failure of the EC-HTE mechanism.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:60:H1b: The exp001 mechanism requires both signed positive error-CATE coupling and subgroup-prior imbalance. A balanced-prior grid may not show global correction worse than uncorrected unless the design explicitly varies group probabilities and reports prior-weighted posterior distortion.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:62:H1c: `bias_bound_score` should be signed or decomposed into signed correlation and magnitude terms. A magnitude-only diagnostic cannot distinguish positive and negative correlation controls and should not be the secondary success criterion for H1 directionality.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:64:H1d: Stage1 correction ablations should remain blocked until the Stage0 correction operator is aligned. Otherwise partial pooling may be judged against an algebraically inconsistent \"stratified\" or \"global\" correction baseline.\n./artifacts/analysis/exp002_negative_mechanism_audit_20260531.md:70:- Replace the direct `P(Z_hat | Z)^T` inverse used for GATE correction with the posterior mixing matrix `P(Z | Z_hat)` or an equivalent prevalence-aware likelihood/EM formulation.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:14:2. Recompute the H1 grid diagnostic using directional, signed subgroup error terms rather than the current magnitude-only bias-bound score.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:19:Registry hypothesis for `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`: a GPU-vectorized DGP grid varying subgroup misclassification heterogeneity and CATE heterogeneity should reproduce the EC-HTE bias-bound mechanism: positive correlation between error and treatment effect should make global correction worse than uncorrected annotations, while negative correlation should not show the same degradation.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:21:Operational H1 grid criterion from `ROADMAP.md`:\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:28:Observed negative result, source `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` registry and `artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/planned_metrics.json`:\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:34:Local condition-metric aggregation from `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` shows the negative Spearman is driven more by grid axes than by positive-vs-negative direction: mean oracle-global-worse rate was 0.671 for low CATE heterogeneity and 0.218 for high CATE heterogeneity; mean rates were 0.442 for positive and 0.447 for negative. This supports a mechanism-audit interpretation rather than \"H1 is globally false.\"\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:40:The exp002 code constructs a row-stochastic transition matrix `C[g,h] = P(Z_hat=h | Z=g)` and applies `solve(C.T, observed_gate)`. For subgroup means/GATEs indexed by observed labels, however, the observed vector is mixed by `P(Z=g | Z_hat=h)`, which depends on subgroup prevalence and column normalization. Directly inverting `C.T` is only harmless in special cases such as uniform prevalence plus doubly stochastic error. Heterogeneous row errors break that condition.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:42:Literature link: matrix/inverse matrix methods are estimand-specific and sensitive to the assumed misclassification model; applying a nondifferential or wrong correction can be worse than the naive estimate.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:44:### F2. Direction-only H1 criterion is too coarse\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:50:The estimated-global minus oracle-global GATE RMSE gap is only 0.000766 in `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`. The failure is therefore not primarily due to expert budget estimation noise. More labels would not fix the current H1 diagnostic or matrix algebra.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:58:### 1. Extended matrix / inverse matrix correction with validation data\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:62:Relevance: Directly matches EC-HTE's use of internal validation labels to correct categorical misclassification. The paper is useful because it emphasizes that matrix correction must match the data-generating misclassification structure, and that an incorrect nondifferential-error assumption can perform worse than the naive estimate.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:64:Design choice/trick from others: use internal validation data to estimate the appropriate correction matrix; separate matrix, inverse-matrix, and likelihood corrections; check whether the assumed nondifferential matrix is valid before applying the inverse.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:76:EC-HTE mapping: partial pooling should pool confusion-matrix logits or predictive probabilities across subgroup/error strata. It should be compared against both global and independent per-subgroup matrices only after the correction target is made prevalence-aware.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:92:Relevance: Already represented in memory.md, but important here because exp002's failure suggests a need for a diagnostic that perturbs categorical labels through a known transition matrix and tests whether extrapolation recovers the intended bias direction.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:94:Design choice/trick from others: add controlled extra misclassification according to a transition matrix, fit the downstream estimator repeatedly, then extrapolate back to the no-error limit.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:96:EC-HTE mapping: use MC-SIMEX as a diagnostic baseline, not primary solution. If MC-SIMEX and posterior-matrix correction disagree under oracle transition matrices, the DGP/estimand implementation is not yet stable enough for Stage 1.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:140:### H-next-001: posterior-matrix correction\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:144:Responds to failure mode: F1 matrix orientation / estimand mismatch.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:154:Test: implement binary/k-class EM baseline on synthetic cells with known transition matrix and uniform/nonuniform prevalences. Success requires EM GATE RMSE < global correction GATE RMSE in positive and negative correlation settings, not only in one hand-picked cell.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:162:Test: compute candidate signed diagnostics from existing `condition_metrics.jsonl`, then confirm on a repaired DGP grid. Success requires Spearman rho >= 0.50 with bootstrap CI lower > 0 for the repaired diagnostic; otherwise H1 should be reframed around estimator-specific algebra rather than error-CATE correlation alone.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:164:### H-next-004: hierarchical confusion-matrix pooling\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:166:Mechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: Under sparse expert validation labels, hierarchical partial pooling over subgroup confusion-matrix logits will reduce GATE RMSE relative to independent per-subgroup confusion matrices by lowering variance, while still improving over global correction when true subgroup errors are heterogeneous.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:182:Mechanistic falsifiable hypothesis: If the corrected H1 mechanism is real, MC-SIMEX extrapolation using the oracle categorical transition matrix will move GATE estimates toward the true GATE vector as added misclassification increases and extrapolates back, whereas the current exp002 inverse correction may move estimates in the wrong direction under heterogeneous row errors.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:186:Test: run MC-SIMEX on a fixed subset of exp002 cells. Success requires extrapolated GATE RMSE < uncorrected GATE RMSE in the same cells where posterior-matrix correction succeeds.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:204:   - matrix recovery error,\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/report/exp002_negative_literature_solutions_20260531.md:214:Decision: pause formal H2 launch. Approve a narrow mechanism-audit patch around H-next-001 through H-next-003. If posterior/EM correction repairs the grid-level relationship, then resume H2 partial-pooling using the repaired correction target. If it does not, revise H1 from \"positive error-CATE correlation makes global correction worse\" to a sharper estimator-specific claim: global correction fails when the posterior observed-label mixing matrix differs materially from the pooled correction matrix in directions aligned with subgroup treatment-effect contrasts.\n./artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/local_dry_run/planned_metrics.json:4: `planned_metrics.json`: planned matrix metrics, each with `target_id`, `row`, `col`, `metric_key`, `exp_id`, and `source`.\n./memory.md:4:EC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\n./memory.md:13:- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\n./memory.md:25:- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\n./memory.md:32:- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\n./memory.md:33:- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\n./memory.md:35:- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\n./memory.md:42:- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\n./memory.md:50:| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\n./memory.md:52:| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\n./memory.md:59:- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\n./memory.md:72:- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/subgroup_variables_available/subgroup_variable_coverage/exp_id=null/source=support_for_variables_pending_linkage: refugee status, eldest-child gender, and parent education are explicitly used in the paper; public CBPS survey metadata supports host/camp and household/individual variables, but transcript-label linkage requires restricted/anonymized data access. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; CBPS https://macmillan.yale.edu/refugee/coxs-bazar-panel-survey; HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33`\n./memory.md:76:- [2026-05-31] \"tool_result\", \"session\": \"feishu:group:oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3\", \"text\": \"---\\nname: resource-saturation\\ndescription: \\\"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\\\"\\nalways: false\\nroles:\\n  - director\\n  - agent\\n---\\n\\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\\n\\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\\n\\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\\n\\n任何\\\"分配了但没跑\\\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\\\"暂无可铺实验\\\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\\n\\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\\n\\n## B. GPU 效率检查\\n\\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\\n\\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\\n\\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\\n- 论文章节起草\\n- 代码 review / refactor\\n\\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\\n\\n## D. 汇报格式\\n\\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\\n\\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / {\"project\": \"ec_hte_codex_a\", \"file\": \"memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory\\n\\n## 项目定位\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\n\\n## 执行约束\\n当前 exp_001_bias_bound_mvp，验证 EC-HTE 的核心机制：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 CATE magnitude 正相关时，pooled/global confusion-matrix correction 会比 uncorrected noisy modifier 产生更大的 subgroup CATE bias。\\n\\n具体产出：\\n1. 在本地项目目录创建可复现代码包，建议路径 `repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/`，包含顶部文件注释和必要 docstring。\\n2. 实现半合成 DGP：k=3 或 k=4 subgroup，T~Bernoulli(0.5)，Y = base + tau_g*T + noise；真实 subgroup G 被 LLM/noisy modifier Z 按 subgroup-specific confusion/error rate 误分类。至少覆盖 positive-correlation 和 negative-correlation 两种配置。\\n3. 比较方法至少包含：uncorrected(Z direct)、global-corrected(pooled confusion matrix)、stratified/oracle subgroup-specific correction。可先用 GATE/subgroup CATE 的 plug-in estimator，若时间允许再加 causal forest/DR-learner接口占位。\\n4. 指标：subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、global correction vs uncorrected 的 bias ratio、diagnostic product = misclassification heterogeneity × CATE heterogeneity。输出 CSV/JSON + 一张 heatmap 或 scatter 图。\\n5. 先 quick run：replications=10，确认脚本可启动；再正式小规模 run：replications>=200，N 至少覆盖 2000 和 5000，预算/混淆矩阵估计可先用 oracle known-confusion，另加有限 expert sample B=500 的估计版本作为可选。\\n6. 更新 registry：运行前/完成后保留 spec，包括 command、data_path='synthetic/generated_by_script'、data_count、model_name='semi_synthetic_gate_estimator'、seed、log_path、artifact paths；完成后根据结果设 conclusion=success/negative，并记录核心 metrics。若 registry DONE 转换要求 checkpoint_path，使用结果 JSON 路径作为 artifact/checkpoint 占位并说明非模型实验。\\n7. 注册 artifact：结果目录和简短报告。\\n\\n环境信息：本地 Python 环境未知，请先用 `python --version`、`pip list` 探测；只安装必要轻量包（numpy/pandas/scipy/matplotlib/seaborn/sklearn）。不要使用 SSH，不要使用 GPU。代码必须可在本地直接运行。\\n\\n验证步骤：\\n- `python repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/run_experiment.py --help`\\n- `python repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/run_experiment.py --quick --replications 10 --out-dir artifacts/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/quick`\\n- 正式运行命令写入 registry spec。\\n\\n出错诊断：若包缺失，优先记录缺失包并最小安装；若 correction 数学导致概率非法，clip 并在报告中说明；若 global correction 未劣于 uncorrected，检查 DGP 是否确实设置为 positive correlation 且 pooled correction 是否产生 under/over 向量化的 EC-HTE DGP grid。扫描 subgroup misclassification heterogeneity、CATE heterogeneity、correlation_direction={positive, negative}，比较 uncorrected noisy modifier、global pooled confusion correction、stratified/oracle correction。核心验证：positive correlation 下 global correction 的 subgroup CATE bias / GATE RMSE 是否高于 uncorrected；negative correlation 下该恶化应减弱或消失。输出 observed bias vs diagnostic product 的 CSV/JSON 和图。\\n\\n执行要求：\\n1. 先做只读/轻量预检：preflight_check、list_available_gpus、df -h、python/conda/torch 探测。确认只使用 cuda:0。记录 GPU 型号、driver、CUDA、torch、磁盘可用空间。\\n2. work_dir `/home/root/ec_hte` 不存在时创建。远程路径作为本任务代码真源：在 `/home/root/ec_hte/experiments/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/` 写代码、日志和输出。\\n3. 环境：优先用已有 conda/base 或系统 python；若 torch 已可用则不安装大包。磁盘只有约 30GB，严禁下载大模型/大数据。最多安装 numpy/pandas/matplotlib/seaborn/scipy 等轻量包；如 torch 不可用，安装与 CUDA 匹配的 PyTorch 但先估算磁盘，空间不足就回报 blocker，不强装。\\n4. 代码必须有文件顶部注释；关键函数 docstring；特殊数学修正（confusion inverse/clip）必须注释原因。\\n5. dry-run：`python run_gpu_dgp_grid.py --quick --out-dir outputs/quick`，确认 `torch.cuda.is_available()` 且 device 为 cuda。\\n6. registry provenance：正式提交前将 exp_002 状态推进到可提交状态（如需 QUEUED 则更新），spec 至少包含 command、server、gpu_ids、data_path='synthetic/generated_by_script'、data_count（grid cells × N × replications）、model_name='pytorch_cuda_dgp_grid'、seed、gpu_model、driver_version、cuda_version、torch_version、hyperparams、log_path、checkpoint_path/result_json_path。\\n7. 正式启动必须调用 heterogeneity small/large；若预计 >2小时，先跑 N={2000,5000}, replications=100 的首批，并在 registry notes 写清楚。\\n\\n验证步骤：\\n- `python run_gpu_dgp_grid.py --help`\\n- quick dry-run 完成并生成 映射与 oracle-vs-estimated correction 对照\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\n\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 把 Stage 0/1 目标落到 experiment matrix：H1 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation 均必须有 target/cell 映射。\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup \\n…[truncated 2204 chars]\", \"tool\": \"mcp__agent_ml_research__read_roadmap\", \"feishu:group:oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3\", \"text\": \"---\\nname: resource-saturation\\ndescription: \\\"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\\\"\\nalways: false\\nroles:\\n  - director\\n  - agent\\n---\\n\\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\\n\\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\\n\\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\\n\\n任何\\\"分配了但没跑\\\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\\\"暂无可铺实验\\\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\\n\\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\\n\\n## B. GPU 效率检查\\n\\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\\n\\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\\n\\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\\n- 论文章节起草\\n- 代码 review / refactor\\n\\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\\n\\n## D. 汇报格式\\n\\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\\n\\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\\n\", \"tool\": \"Bash\", \"elapsed_ms\": 0}\n./logs/chats/feishu_group_oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3.jsonl:158:{\"ts\": subsample B 至少包含 100/500 或一个固定 B=500。\\n- correction algebra 要清楚注释：oracle global 使用真实 pooled confusion，estimated global 使用 expert subsample 的估计 confusion；如果 confusion inverse 后概率/计数非法，clip/renormalize N=2000、rep=200 的 matrix-critical run。\\n\\n出错诊断：若 global 不 worse，优先检查 positive correlation DGP、metric 定义和 oracle-vs-estimated split，不要直接推进其他实验。负向结果必须在 registry 标记 negative 并等待 \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat\\n…[truncated 10565 chars]\", \"tool\":
